<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0001 -->
# 六级单词 0001 · 废除，取消
中文：v. 废除，取消
单词：###abolish###
搭配：###abolish a law### 废除法律
例句：The government decided to ###abolish### the outdated regulation, which had caused dissatisfaction among young entrepreneurs.
译文：政府决定废除这项过时的规定，它曾引起年轻创业者的不满。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0002 -->
# 六级单词 0002 · 过时的
中文：adj. 过时的
单词：###outdated###
搭配：###outdated equipment### 过时的设备
例句：The government decided to abolish the ###outdated### regulation, which had caused dissatisfaction among young entrepreneurs.
译文：政府决定废除这项过时的规定，它曾引起年轻创业者的不满。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0003 -->
# 六级单词 0003 · 不满
中文：n. 不满
单词：###dissatisfaction###
搭配：###express dissatisfaction with### 对……表示不满
例句：The government decided to abolish the outdated regulation, which had caused ###dissatisfaction### among young entrepreneurs.
译文：政府决定废除这项过时的规定，它曾引起年轻创业者的不满。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a young entrepreneur### 年轻的创业者

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0005 -->
# 六级单词 0005 · 缩写，缩略词
中文：n. 缩写，缩略词
单词：###abbreviation###
搭配：###an abbreviation for ……### 的缩写
例句：Could you explain the ###abbreviation### in this footnote? Its meaning seems ambiguous, and the glossary doesn't include it.
译文：你能解释一下这个脚注里的缩写吗？它的意思好像很含糊，词汇表里也没有。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0006 -->
# 六级单词 0006 · 脚注
中文：n. 脚注
单词：###footnote###
搭配：###add a footnote### 加脚注
例句：Could you explain the abbreviation in this ###footnote###? Its meaning seems ambiguous, and the glossary doesn't include it.
译文：你能解释一下这个脚注里的缩写吗？它的意思好像很含糊，词汇表里也没有。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0007 -->

译文：你能解释一下这个脚注里的缩写吗？它的意思好像很含糊，词汇表里也没有。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0008 -->

# 六级单词 0009 · 电工
中文：n. 电工
单词：###electrician###
搭配：###call an electrician### 叫电工
例句：Had the ###electrician### not noticed the leakage in time, the boiler might have exploded and caused heavy casualties.
译文：要不是电工及时发现了泄漏，锅炉可能已经爆炸，造成重大伤亡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 六级单词 0013 · 过度地，过分地
中文：adv. 过度地，过分地
单词：###excessively###
搭配：###excessively long### 过长的
例句：Spending ###excessively### long hours on screens may impair children's cognitive development and aggravate their sleep problems.
译文：长时间过度看屏幕可能损害儿童的认知发展，并加重他们的睡眠问题。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0014 -->

中文：n. 人口普查

# 六级单词 0045 · 大幅度地，急剧地
中文：adv. 大幅度地，急剧地

搭配：###drastically reduce### 大幅减少
例句：Not only did the drought ###drastically### diminish the harvest, but it also drove up the price of barley and poultry.

搭配：###kitchen utensils### 厨房用具

<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：别这么咄咄逼人，跟经理唱反调解决不了分歧，还是讲点策略吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0618 -->
# 六级单词 0618 · 征服者

搭配：###a foreign conqueror### 外来征服者
例句：Chronicles written by ###conquerors### often glorify their own deeds and disregard the massacres they committed.
译文：征服者写的史书往往美化自己的功绩，对他们犯下的屠杀罪行却视而不见。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Don't be so cynical: not every charity is a ###fraud###, and plenty of people still act out of decency and courtesy.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0830 -->

例句：Many graduates sense a ###disconnect### between their educational background and what employers demand, which breeds frustration and leaves them disillusioned.
译文：许多毕业生觉得所受的教育和雇主的要求脱节，这让他们沮丧，也让他们感到幻灭。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0873 -->

单词：###economics###
搭配：###behavioral economics### 行为经济学
例句：Ideally, consumers would weigh every option, but behavioral ###economics### shows they often make irrational choices out of inertia.
译文：理想情况下，消费者会权衡每个选项，但行为经济学表明，他们常常出于惯性做出不理性的选择。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：把馅料抹均匀，这样南瓜派就不会烤焦，然后在上面撒些坚果仁，再加点奶泡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###the fog dissipated### 雾散了

译文：虽然暴风雨一夜之间就消散了，山体滑坡的威胁仍然存在，居民被告知远离陡坡附近。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1095 -->
# 六级单词 1095 · 耸人听闻的；轰动的

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1115 -->
# 六级单词 1115 · 专门知识，专长
中文：n. 专门知识，专长
单词：###expertise###
搭配：###technical expertise### 技术专长
例句：Few local clinics have the ###expertise### to diagnose rare syndromes, so patients with physiological symptoms like sudden paralysis are referred elsewhere.
译文：很少有地方诊所具备诊断罕见综合征的专业能力，所以出现突然瘫痪等生理症状的病人会被转诊到别处。

# 六级单词 1125 · 全部曲目，全部本领
中文：n. 全部曲目，全部本领
单词：###repertoire###
搭配：###a wide repertoire### 曲目丰富
例句：Your daughter is extraordinarily gifted; she plays the harp like a professional, and her ###repertoire### is a marvel for her age.
译文：你女儿天赋超群，竖琴弹得像专业演奏者一样，以她的年纪，会的曲目之多真让人惊叹。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1126 -->

译文：你女儿天赋超群，竖琴弹得像专业演奏者一样，以她的年纪，会的曲目之多真让人惊叹。

# 六级单词 1127 · 似乎合理的，可信的

单词：###plausible###
搭配：###a plausible explanation### 似乎合理的解释
例句：The results seemed ###plausible### until close scrutiny revealed that the postdoctoral researcher's data were a fabrication.
译文：那些结果起初看似可信，直到仔细审查才发现那位博士后研究员的数据是捏造的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###blind obedience### 盲从

译文：为了培养孩子的性情，父母应当把温情与坚定结合起来，而不是要求孩子盲目服从。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1167 -->
# 六级单词 1167 · 裂变
中文：n. 裂变
单词：###fission###
搭配：###nuclear fission### 核裂变
例句：Nuclear ###fission### occurs when a neutron splits a uranium atom, whereas fusion joins light atoms and releases even more energy.
译文：当一个中子把铀原子撞裂时就会发生核裂变，而核聚变是把轻原子结合起来，释放出更多的能量。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 六级单词 1255 · 使合法化

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1334 -->
# 六级单词 1334 · 报销，偿还
中文：n. 报销，偿还
单词：###reimbursement###
搭配：###travel reimbursement### 差旅费报销
例句：It was the accountant's incompetence that caused payroll errors and delayed ###reimbursement###, which staff deeply resent.
译文：正是会计的无能导致工资单出错、报销延误，这让员工非常不满。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1335 -->
# 六级单词 1335 · 怨恨，对……不满
中文：v. 怨恨，对……不满
单词：###resent###
搭配：###resent being treated unfairly### 对不公对待感到不满
例句：It was the accountant's incompetence that caused payroll errors and delayed reimbursement, which staff deeply ###resent###.
译文：正是会计的无能导致工资单出错、报销延误，这让员工非常不满。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1336 -->
# 六级单词 1336 · 无能的，不称职的
中文：adj. 无能的，不称职的
单词：###incompetent###
搭配：###an incompetent manager### 无能的经理
例句：The repairman was so ###incompetent### that our radiator leaked even more, and when I rebuked him, he refused to rectify it.
译文：那个维修工太无能了，我们的暖气片反而漏得更厉害；我责备他，他还拒绝返工。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1337 -->

搭配：###a car radiator### 汽车散热器
例句：The repairman was so incompetent that our ###radiator### leaked even more, and when I rebuked him, he refused to rectify it.
译文：那个维修工太无能了，我们的暖气片反而漏得更厉害；我责备他，他还拒绝返工。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###rebuke sb. for### 因……责备某人
例句：The repairman was so incompetent that our radiator leaked even more, and when I ###rebuked### him, he refused to rectify it.

搭配：###rectify a mistake### 改正错误
例句：The repairman was so incompetent that our radiator leaked even more, and when I rebuked him, he refused to ###rectify### it.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1340 -->
# 六级单词 1340 · 招致，承担
中文：v. 招致，承担
单词：###incur###
搭配：###incur costs### 产生费用
例句：Borrowers who miss repayments ###incur### penalties on the remainder of their loans, pay higher premiums and may never regain good credit.
译文：不按时还款的借款人要为剩余贷款支付罚金，缴纳更高的保险费，信用也可能再也恢复不了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1341 -->

搭配：###the remainder of### 剩下的
例句：Borrowers who miss repayments incur penalties on the ###remainder### of their loans, pay higher premiums and may never regain good credit.
译文：不按时还款的借款人要为剩余贷款支付罚金，缴纳更高的保险费，信用也可能再也恢复不了。

# 六级单词 1344 · 不加区别地，随意地

单词：###indiscriminately###
搭配：###use indiscriminately### 滥用
例句：Don't take antibiotics ###indiscriminately###: they kill helpful microscopic organisms and leave you susceptible to infections that necessitate stronger drugs.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1345 -->

例句：Builders insulate walls and roofs at minimal cost, and good insulation saves thousands of ###kilowatt###-hours a year.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 六级单词 1418 · 诽谤
中文：n. 诽谤
单词：###slander###
搭配：###sue for slander### 以诽谤罪起诉
例句：Professional jealousy can breed malice and ###slander###, so managers should reward merit openly and suppress favoritism.
译文：职场上的嫉妒会滋生恶意和诽谤，所以管理者应公开奖励有功之人，并杜绝偏袒。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1420 -->
# 六级单词 1420 · 哺乳动物
中文：n. 哺乳动物
单词：###mammal###
搭配：###marine mammals### 海洋哺乳动物
例句：Whether testing drugs on ###mammals### is justifiable remains contested: public sentiment calls it unethical, while scientists defend it.
译文：用哺乳动物做药物试验是否合理至今仍有争议：公众情感上认为这不道德，而科学家则为它辩护。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###write in prose### 用散文写

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1505 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1533 -->

搭配：###a merciful judge### 宽厚的法官
例句：Had the judge not been ###merciful###, the young pirate who pleaded guilty would have spent years in prison, which his minor offence did not warrant.

译文：别再像个势利眼一样躲在角落里喝果汁了，去跟客人们打成一片吧，哪怕是那些老于世故的银行家。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1553 -->

搭配：###systematically excluded### 系统性地被排斥
例句：Unless groups that are ###systematically### underrepresented gain fair access to education, social mobility will stall and segregation will persist.
译文：除非长期以来代表性不足的群体能公平地接受教育，否则社会流动会停滞，隔离现象也会持续。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1565 -->
# 六级单词 1565 · 垄断
中文：v. 垄断
单词：###monopolize###
搭配：###monopolize the market### 垄断市场
例句：Should a firm ###monopolize### a market, regulators may break up the monopoly or veto mergers, even if it outperforms its rivals.
译文：如果一家公司垄断了市场，即便它确实比对手出色，监管机构也可能拆分这家垄断企业或否决其并购。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Ten pounds for a coffee? That's ###monstrous###, even shameful; no wonder locals scoff at the place. It's senseless!

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1585 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1621 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1622 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1625 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1626 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1627 -->

例句：In retrospect, his rash investment reduced his savings to ###nought###, which explains his reluctance to take risks today.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1628 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1629 -->
# 六级单词 1629 · 允许的，许可的
中文：adj. 允许的，许可的
单词：###permissible###
搭配：###permissible levels### 允许的标准
例句：What is deemed ###permissible### in one subculture may be regarded as obscene in another, so young people online are prone to misunderstandings.
译文：在一种亚文化中被认为可以接受的东西，在另一种亚文化中可能被视为下流，因此年轻人在网上容易产生误解。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1630 -->

搭配：###youth subculture### 青年亚文化
例句：What is deemed permissible in one ###subculture### may be regarded as obscene in another, so young people online are prone to misunderstandings.
译文：在一种亚文化中被认为可以接受的东西，在另一种亚文化中可能被视为下流，因此年轻人在网上容易产生误解。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1631 -->
# 六级单词 1631 · 下流的，淫秽的

搭配：###obscene language### 下流话
例句：What is deemed permissible in one subculture may be regarded as ###obscene### in another, so young people online are prone to misunderstandings.
译文：在一种亚文化中被认为可以接受的东西，在另一种亚文化中可能被视为下流，因此年轻人在网上容易产生误解。

译文：在动物园里我们看到了一只黑豹、一条来自亚马孙的巨大爬行动物和一只啄木鸟，不过叫得最响的是那只公鸡！

单词：###severity###
搭配：###the severity of### 的严重程度
例句：On the plateau, the scarcity of oxygen increases the ###severity### of altitude sickness once climbers cross a certain threshold.
译文：在高原上，一旦登山者越过一定的海拔临界点，缺氧会加重高原反应的严重程度。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1730 -->
# 六级单词 1730 · 前提
中文：n. 前提
单词：###premise###
搭配：###on the premise that### 以……为前提
例句：The ###premise### of the plan being weak, we cannot presume it is practicable, nor should we preclude other options.
译文：由于这个计划的前提站不住脚，我们不能想当然地认为它可行，也不应排除其他方案。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1731 -->

搭配：###presume that### 假定
例句：The premise of the plan being weak, we cannot ###presume### it is practicable, nor should we preclude other options.

搭配：###a practicable plan### 可行的计划
例句：The premise of the plan being weak, we cannot presume it is ###practicable###, nor should we preclude other options.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1734 -->

中文：v. 维护，支持
单词：###uphold###
搭配：###uphold the law### 维护法律
例句：The new judge vowed to ###uphold### the precedent set by her predecessor, so that her successor would inherit a consistent legal tradition.
译文：新任法官承诺维护前任确立的判例，以便她的继任者能继承一个一致的法律传统。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1735 -->

搭配：###set a precedent### 开先例

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1736 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1738 -->
# 六级单词 1738 · 促成，突然引起
中文：v. 促成，突然引起
单词：###precipitate###
搭配：###precipitate a crisis### 引发危机
例句：Don't ###precipitate### a quarrel with unreasonable demands; expecting him to agree overnight is wishful thinking and unrealistic.
译文：别提出不合理的要求而引发争吵；指望他一夜之间就同意，完全是一厢情愿、不切实际。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1739 -->

搭配：###unreasonable demands### 不合理的要求
例句：Don't precipitate a quarrel with ###unreasonable### demands; expecting him to agree overnight is wishful thinking and unrealistic.
译文：别提出不合理的要求而引发争吵；指望他一夜之间就同意，完全是一厢情愿、不切实际。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###wishful thinking### 一厢情愿的想法
例句：Don't precipitate a quarrel with unreasonable demands; expecting him to agree overnight is ###wishful### thinking and unrealistic.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1742 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1743 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1746 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1750 -->
# 六级单词 1750 · 制片人；生产者
中文：n. 制片人；生产者
单词：###producer###
搭配：###a film producer### 电影制片人
例句：The film ###producer### paid royalties to use the trademark, a tradeoff that raised costs but avoided lawsuits.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1751 -->

例句：The film producer paid ###royalties### to use the trademark, a tradeoff that raised costs but avoided lawsuits.

例句：The film producer paid royalties to use the trademark, a ###tradeoff### that raised costs but avoided lawsuits.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1754 -->
# 六级单词 1754 · 繁殖；传播
中文：v. 繁殖；传播
单词：###propagate###
搭配：###propagate plants### 繁殖植物
例句：To ###propagate### the rose, take a cutting; your last cuts were uneven, so prune it carefully or the stems won't thicken.
译文：要繁殖这株玫瑰，就剪一根插条；你上次剪得不齐，这次要仔细修剪，不然茎长不粗。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1755 -->

搭配：###uneven ground### 不平的地面
例句：To propagate the rose, take a cutting; your last cuts were ###uneven###, so prune it carefully or the stems won't thicken.
译文：要繁殖这株玫瑰，就剪一根插条；你上次剪得不齐，这次要仔细修剪，不然茎长不粗。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1756 -->

单词：###prune###
搭配：###prune a tree### 修剪树木
例句：To propagate the rose, take a cutting; your last cuts were uneven, so ###prune### it carefully or the stems won't thicken.
译文：要繁殖这株玫瑰，就剪一根插条；你上次剪得不齐，这次要仔细修剪，不然茎长不粗。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1757 -->

单词：###thicken###
搭配：###the sauce thickens### 酱汁变稠
例句：To propagate the rose, take a cutting; your last cuts were uneven, so prune it carefully or the stems won't ###thicken###.
译文：要繁殖这株玫瑰，就剪一根插条；你上次剪得不齐，这次要仔细修剪，不然茎长不粗。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1758 -->
# 六级单词 1758 · 繁殖；传播
中文：n. 繁殖；传播
单词：###propagation###
搭配：###plant propagation### 植物繁殖
例句：Some plants spread by root ###propagation### instead of seed reproduction, and they can regenerate after fires, preventing the recurrence of erosion.
译文：有些植物靠根繁殖而不是种子繁殖，它们在火灾后能重新生长，防止水土流失再次发生。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1759 -->
# 六级单词 1759 · 繁殖，生殖；复制
中文：n. 繁殖，生殖；复制
单词：###reproduction###
搭配：###sexual reproduction### 有性生殖
例句：Some plants spread by root propagation instead of seed ###reproduction###, and they can regenerate after fires, preventing the recurrence of erosion.
译文：有些植物靠根繁殖而不是种子繁殖，它们在火灾后能重新生长，防止水土流失再次发生。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1760 -->

搭配：###a wholly owned subsidiary### 全资子公司

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1770 -->
# 六级单词 1770 · 可以量化的
中文：adj. 可以量化的
单词：###quantifiable###
搭配：###quantifiable results### 可量化的结果
例句：Although the effect of noise is ###quantifiable###, studies reveal that stress, the factor proximately responsible, is what really retards learning and makes revision wasteful.
译文：尽管噪音的影响可以量化，但研究显示，压力这个直接相关的因素才是真正阻碍学习、让复习白费力气的原因。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1771 -->

译文：尽管噪音的影响可以量化，但研究显示，压力这个直接相关的因素才是真正阻碍学习、让复习白费力气的原因。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Only after the harsh law was ###repealed### did the repression end, and investors once repelled by it returned to fund regenerative farming.

例句：The novel blends romance with adventure, featuring a savage ###serpent### that hides in a shadowy forest.
译文：这部小说把爱情和冒险融为一体，书中有一条凶猛的大蛇潜伏在阴暗的森林里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a shadowy figure### 模糊的身影

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1803 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1806 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1810 -->

中文：v. 隔离，分开
单词：###segregate###
搭配：###segregate A from B### 把 A 与 B 分开
例句：That the city no longer ###segregates### schools by income signifies a more unified society, though uninformed critics doubt it.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1812 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1814 -->

例句：In that western, the ###sheriff### stuns a vicious, willful gang leader with one punch, and the whole town cheers.

例句：In that western, the sheriff stuns a ###vicious###, willful gang leader with one punch, and the whole town cheers.

搭配：###a willful child### 任性的孩子
例句：In that western, the sheriff stuns a vicious, ###willful### gang leader with one punch, and the whole town cheers.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1822 -->

搭配：###wring out a towel### 拧干毛巾
例句：###Wring### out the wet underwear before hanging it, don't let the clothes tangle, and pick up soy sauce and staple foods on your way home.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1827 -->
# 六级单词 1827 · 想象，使形象化
中文：v. 想象，使形象化
单词：###visualize###
搭配：###visualize the future### 设想未来
例句：To help students ###visualize### molecules, the teacher explained that a subscript shows the number of atoms and that viscous liquids mix less uniformly.
译文：为了帮助学生想象分子的样子，老师解释说下标表示原子的数目，而黏稠的液体混合得不太均匀。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1828 -->

中文：n. 下标
单词：###subscript###
搭配：###a chemical subscript### 化学式下标
例句：To help students visualize molecules, the teacher explained that a ###subscript### shows the number of atoms and that viscous liquids mix less uniformly.
译文：为了帮助学生想象分子的样子，老师解释说下标表示原子的数目，而黏稠的液体混合得不太均匀。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1829 -->

中文：adj. 黏稠的
单词：###viscous###
搭配：###viscous liquid### 黏稠的液体
例句：To help students visualize molecules, the teacher explained that a subscript shows the number of atoms and that ###viscous### liquids mix less uniformly.
译文：为了帮助学生想象分子的样子，老师解释说下标表示原子的数目，而黏稠的液体混合得不太均匀。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1830 -->

中文：adv. 均匀地，一律
单词：###uniformly###
搭配：###uniformly distributed### 均匀分布的
例句：To help students visualize molecules, the teacher explained that a subscript shows the number of atoms and that viscous liquids mix less ###uniformly###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1831 -->
# 六级单词 1831 · 打（谷），脱粒
中文：v. 打（谷），脱粒
单词：###thresh###
搭配：###thresh wheat### 打麦子
例句：After a day of ###threshing### wheat, wielding a heavy hammer and driving wedges into logs, I feel a terrible stiffness in my shoulders.
译文：打了一天麦子、抡大锤、往木头里打楔子，我的肩膀僵硬得厉害。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1832 -->

中文：v. 挥舞；行使
单词：###wield###
搭配：###wield power### 行使权力
例句：After a day of threshing wheat, ###wielding### a heavy hammer and driving wedges into logs, I feel a terrible stiffness in my shoulders.
译文：打了一天麦子、抡大锤、往木头里打楔子，我的肩膀僵硬得厉害。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1833 -->
# 六级单词 1833 · 楔子
中文：n. 楔子
单词：###wedge###
搭配：###drive a wedge### 打入楔子
例句：After a day of threshing wheat, wielding a heavy hammer and driving ###wedges### into logs, I feel a terrible stiffness in my shoulders.
译文：打了一天麦子、抡大锤、往木头里打楔子，我的肩膀僵硬得厉害。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1834 -->

中文：n. 僵硬
单词：###stiffness###
搭配：###muscle stiffness### 肌肉僵硬
例句：After a day of threshing wheat, wielding a heavy hammer and driving wedges into logs, I feel a terrible ###stiffness### in my shoulders.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1835 -->
# 六级单词 1835 · 制服，平定
中文：v. 制服，平定
单词：###subdue###
搭配：###subdue a revolt### 平定叛乱
例句：The army ###subdued### the rebellion, and the government successively announced the suspension of the travel ban and the release of prisoners, claiming military superiority.
译文：军队平定了叛乱，政府随后相继宣布暂停出行禁令、释放囚犯，宣称已取得军事优势。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1836 -->

中文：adv. 相继地，依次地
单词：###successively###
搭配：###successively announce### 相继宣布
例句：The army subdued the rebellion, and the government ###successively### announced the suspension of the travel ban and the release of prisoners, claiming military superiority.
译文：军队平定了叛乱，政府随后相继宣布暂停出行禁令、释放囚犯，宣称已取得军事优势。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1837 -->
# 六级单词 1837 · 暂停，中止
中文：n. 暂停，中止
单词：###suspension###
搭配：###the suspension of### 的暂停
例句：The army subdued the rebellion, and the government successively announced the ###suspension### of the travel ban and the release of prisoners, claiming military superiority.
译文：军队平定了叛乱，政府随后相继宣布暂停出行禁令、释放囚犯，宣称已取得军事优势。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1838 -->
# 六级单词 1838 · 优势，优越性
中文：n. 优势，优越性
单词：###superiority###
搭配：###military superiority### 军事优势
例句：The army subdued the rebellion, and the government successively announced the suspension of the travel ban and the release of prisoners, claiming military ###superiority###.
译文：军队平定了叛乱，政府随后相继宣布暂停出行禁令、释放囚犯，宣称已取得军事优势。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1839 -->
# 六级单词 1839 · 穿过，横越
中文：v. 穿过，横越
单词：###traverse###
搭配：###traverse a desert### 穿越沙漠
例句：The road ###traverses### a mountain pass and tapers to a single lane; the tar is fresh, so drive slowly, as the delay will be transient.
译文：这条路穿过一个山口，越来越窄，最后只剩一条车道；沥青是新铺的，所以慢点开，堵车只是暂时的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1840 -->
# 六级单词 1840 · 逐渐变细
中文：v. 逐渐变细
单词：###taper###
搭配：###taper to a point### 逐渐变尖
例句：The road traverses a mountain pass and ###tapers### to a single lane; the tar is fresh, so drive slowly, as the delay will be transient.
译文：这条路穿过一个山口，越来越窄，最后只剩一条车道；沥青是新铺的，所以慢点开，堵车只是暂时的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1841 -->
# 六级单词 1841 · 沥青，焦油
中文：n. 沥青，焦油
单词：###tar###
搭配：###fresh tar### 新铺的沥青
例句：The road traverses a mountain pass and tapers to a single lane; the ###tar### is fresh, so drive slowly, as the delay will be transient.
译文：这条路穿过一个山口，越来越窄，最后只剩一条车道；沥青是新铺的，所以慢点开，堵车只是暂时的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1842 -->
# 六级单词 1842 · 短暂的，转瞬即逝的
中文：adj. 短暂的，转瞬即逝的
单词：###transient###
搭配：###a transient phenomenon### 短暂的现象
例句：The road traverses a mountain pass and tapers to a single lane; the tar is fresh, so drive slowly, as the delay will be ###transient###.
译文：这条路穿过一个山口，越来越窄，最后只剩一条车道；沥青是新铺的，所以慢点开，堵车只是暂时的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1843 -->
# 六级单词 1843 · 凭借，借以
中文：adv. 凭借，借以
单词：###whereby###
搭配：###a system whereby### 凭借……的制度
例句：The contract specifies the terms ###whereby### each tradesman is paid, and any breach thereof shall void the agreement; therein lies its strength.
译文：合同规定了每位工匠获得报酬的条款，任何违反条款的行为都将使协议失效；合同的效力正在于此。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1848 -->
# 六级单词 1848 · 象征，标志
中文：n. 象征，标志
单词：###token###
搭配：###as a token of### 作为……的象征
例句：As a ###token### of gratitude for doubling the turnover, the firm offered staff unlimited coffee, which proved to have undesirable effects on sleep.
译文：为了感谢员工让营业额翻了一番，公司给员工无限量供应咖啡，结果这对睡眠产生了不良影响。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 六级单词 1852 · 不适合的，不健康的
中文：adj. 不适合的，不健康的
单词：###unfit###
搭配：###unfit for work### 不适合工作
例句：Before the trip, the doctor vaccinated me and gave verbal instructions; I still feel ###unfit### to travel, so let's see how things unfold.
译文：出发前医生给我打了疫苗，还口头交代了注意事项；我还是觉得身体不太适合出行，先看看情况怎么发展吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1856 -->
# 六级单词 1856 · 利用
中文：n. 利用
单词：###utilization###
搭配：###the utilization of resources### 资源利用
例句：Through better ###utilization### of space, new vents and transverse beams, the old mill gave its weavers a brighter workshop.
译文：通过更好地利用空间、安装新的通风口和横梁，这座老纺织厂为织工们提供了更明亮的车间。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1860 -->
# 六级单词 1860 · 发自内心的，本能的
中文：adj. 发自内心的，本能的
单词：###visceral###
搭配：###a visceral reaction### 本能反应
例句：So ###visceral### was the public's wrath that the minister could no longer withhold the truth, and his excuses seemed utterly hollow.
译文：公众的怒火如此强烈，部长再也无法隐瞒真相，他的借口显得空洞至极。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

中文：n. 别墅
单词：###villa###
搭配：###a seaside villa### 海边别墅
例句：Our ###villa### sits beside a virgin forest, where farmers still yoke cattle and miners once dug a vein of silver.
译文：我们住的别墅紧挨着一片原始森林，那里的农民至今还给牛套上牛轭耕地，矿工们曾在这里挖过一条银矿脉。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1868 -->
# 六级单词 1868 · 借口，托词
中文：n. 借口，托词
单词：###pretext###
搭配：###under the pretext of### 以……为借口
例句：Under the ###pretext### of efficiency, the developer subdivided the land into tiny plots, until the market was so saturated that the housing crisis recurred.
译文：开发商以提高效率为借口，把土地分割成一块块小地皮，结果市场饱和到了极点，住房危机再次出现。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1869 -->

译文：开发商以提高效率为借口，把土地分割成一块块小地皮，结果市场饱和到了极点，住房危机再次出现。
